- **quark-gluon-jet-discrimination-eval**: This benchmark evaluates a model's ability to classify high-energy physics particle jets as originating from quarks or gluons using pixelized detector data. Use when the user wants to benchmark on Simulated CMS LHC Jet Data (DELPHES), or asks about evaluating this task. Reports AUC.
